| AC-367 | Control is off until the teacher turns it on, and the mouse goes through it | `packages/excalidraw/tests/panelControl.test.tsx` | A new panel has Control off and sends no input and no control message; turning it on tells the host, lights the switch and puts the view in the mode of control; with it on a press, a release, two quick presses (a double click has no event of its own), a drag (moves with a button down), a context press and the wheel reach the host as events with the window's fractions, the button and the modifiers, and a press or a wheel in a bar beside the picture is not sent; a move is not sent more often than the limit; with it off again nothing is sent. Tests: `AC-367 Control is off in a new panel and nothing is sent`, `AC-367 turning Control on tells the host and lights the switch`, `AC-367 the mouse reaches the host as events with the window's places`, `AC-367 a move is not sent more often than the limit`, `AC-367 nothing is sent once Control is off`. **AMENDED (wave 9a, D-337):** with Control on the view has the keyboard at once (engaged), with no click. |
| AC-368 | the keys go only when the view has the keyboard, and the teacher can take it back | `packages/excalidraw/tests/panelControl.test.tsx` | With Control on and the view clicked, a key, a shortcut and a printed character reach the host as key events with their modifiers and the canvas does not see them; with Control on and the view not clicked the keys go to the canvas as before; the chord (Ctrl+Alt+Home) is not sent and gives the view's keyboard back; the Windows key and the keys the machine keeps are not sent; what an input method committed goes as text events of at most 256 characters each, and a paste is Ctrl+V - a key - so the editor pastes what the machine's clipboard holds. Tests: `AC-368 a key reaches the host and not the canvas when the view has the keyboard`, `AC-368 the keys stay with the canvas until the view is clicked`, `AC-368 the chord gives the keyboard back and is not sent`, `AC-368 the keys the machine keeps are not sent`, `AC-368 a text an input method committed goes as bounded text events`. |
| AC-369 | the protocol carries the control as a closed list | `packages/vscode-bridge/tests/bridge.test.ts` | The messages `editor:control`, `editor:input` and `editor:window` are in the list of messages and round-trip; an input event is one of four kinds and any other is refused, a place outside 0..1, a number that is not finite, a button that is not one of three, a key that is not in the table and a text of more than 256 characters or with control characters are refused; the capture's descriptor carries the control's route, its state and its reason. Tests: `AC-369 the control's messages are in the protocol and round-trip`, `AC-369 an input event is one of four kinds and the others are refused`, `AC-369 a malformed input event is refused`, `AC-369 the descriptor carries the control's state`. |
| AC-370 | the extension accepts input only while Control is on, and within its limits | `packages/vscode-bridge/tests/windowInput.test.ts` | The gate drops every event while Control is off and when the connection ends turns itself off; with Control on it passes an event once and not more often than the limit a second, and says how many it dropped; the keys the machine keeps (the Windows key, Alt+Tab, Alt+Esc, Ctrl+Esc, Alt+F4) never reach the helper; an event that is refused by the protocol never reaches it. Tests: `AC-370 nothing goes through while Control is off`, `AC-370 Control turns itself off when the connection ends`, `AC-370 events above the limit are dropped and counted`, `AC-370 the keys the machine keeps never reach the helper`. |
| AC-371 | an event becomes the helper's line, exactly | `packages/vscode-bridge/tests/windowInput.test.ts` | A pointer, a wheel, a key and a text event are written as one line each with the fields the helper reads (the place as a fraction, the button, the modifier mask, the key's code from the table, the character, the text in base64); a printed character goes as a character, Enter, Tab and Backspace and the arrows go as keys, a line feed in a text goes as Enter; a modifier is a mask and never a key of its own on a line; and the helper is run with the rule (the process and the title) and the route as arguments. Tests: `AC-371 a pointer event is one line`, `AC-371 a printed character is a character and Enter is a key`, `AC-371 a modifier is a mask`, `AC-371 a text goes as base64 and a line feed as Enter`, `AC-371 the helper is run with the rule and the route`. |
| AC-372 | the route is chosen, and the panel is told which | `packages/vscode-bridge/tests/windowInput.test.ts` | With the route `auto` the helper's answer decides (a window with a page to post to is the background route, one without is the focus route); `background` and `focus` force it; the answer and the reason are in the capture's descriptor and are published again when they change; a helper that says the window is not there turns the control to "no window" with that reason and does not exit; a helper that died is started again. Tests: `AC-372 auto chooses by what the window can take`, `AC-372 a setting forces the route`, `AC-372 the descriptor says the route and why`, `AC-372 no window is said and the helper stays`, `AC-372 a dead helper is started again`. |
| AC-373 | a window can be parked and brought back, measured | `packages/vscode-bridge/tests/windowPark.test.ts` | Parking finds the window by rule and moves it far off every display without taking the focus; the answer is the measured place and a move that did not happen is not reported as one; a maximised window is un-maximised first and comes back maximised; the way the window was is kept as facts (no handle) and a bring back with none puts it on the primary display; nothing parks unless it is asked for, a minimised window is left alone and said, and a window parked when the extension ends is brought back. Tests: `AC-373 the window is parked far off every display`, `AC-373 a park that did not happen is not reported as one`, `AC-373 a maximised window comes back maximised`, `AC-373 with nothing kept the window comes back on the primary display`, `AC-373 nothing parks by itself and a minimised window is left alone`, `AC-373 a parked window is brought back when the extension ends`. |
| AC-374 | nothing uses or ships the virtual display driver | `packages/vscode-bridge/tests/windowPark.test.ts` | The extension's source has no display-finding code, no placement on a virtual display and no command that names one; the manifest has the parking commands and no virtual display command; the helper of the display is not a file of the extension; the installer's payload does not list the driver or its control app and its setup does not install them; the install report does not check the driver. Tests: `AC-374 the extension has no virtual display code or command`, `AC-374 the installer neither carries nor installs the driver`, `AC-374 the install report does not check the driver`. |
| AC-375 | the app's session sends the control and says when it ends | `excalidraw-app/tests/bridgeSession.test.ts` | The session sends `editor:control` when the panel turns Control on or off, `editor:input` for an event and `editor:window` for park and bring back, only while connected (a send with no connection is dropped and not queued); a lost connection puts every panel's Control off and the panel is told; the descriptor's route, state and reason come back to the panel. Tests: `AC-377 the capture sees the whole window at any display scaling`. |

Tests: `AC-378 the first descriptor already carries the control, off`, `AC-378 the extension announces the control when it starts`. |
| AC-379 | the session token is kept, and a configured one is not | `packages/vscode-bridge/tests/bridge.test.ts` | A token the extension made is read from the editor's secret storage and used again, a configured token wins and is never kept, a kept value that is not 48 hexadecimal characters is not trusted, and a token that was made is stored once; the extension waits for the storage before it listens, and the notification says the token is kept. Tests: `AC-379 a kept token is used again`, `AC-379 a configured token wins and is never kept`, `AC-379 the extension reads the storage before it listens and keeps what it makes`. |

Tests: `AC-380 a refused token is said and not retried`, `AC-380 any other close is still retried`, `AC-380 the app shows a refused token as rejected`. |

Tests: `AC-445`. |
| AC-446 | the look of the animation is in the file | `packages/excalidraw/tests/flowExportLook.test.ts` | The target of the highlight decides what is lit (the shape, the box, the letters, two of them, and the shape for a step with no label); the letters are the label's own words, font and place, in the text's colour, then the shape's, then the accent; a neon text has the core and the glow and a plain one has neither; a focus grows the letters and a pulse leaves them; the look's neon arrows are three halos of one thin line along each arrow, in the arrow's colour, then the mark's, then the accent, and the halos grow; an arrow is handed to the canvas as it is when the look leaves it, not at all when it is hidden or drawn as a tube, and recoloured when it has a colour, and the element on the page is never changed (D-383). Tests: `AC-446`. |

The camera is the same for the same moment and the same again one round later, flies from the step before and has arrived by the end of the step, shows the current step whole and nearer than the whole chart, takes the shorter of a second and a half and the step, does not move for a chart of one step, and looks at the part of the page the picture is. Each moment is got ready before it is read, for every format, and the encoder is given the way to get ready. Without the camera the chart is drawn once and the source has no flight; with it nothing is drawn up front and the chart is drawn for each camera, at its scale. The panel's choice starts as the bar's setting, changes the export and not the bar, and is locked while the export works (D-384, D-385). Tests: `AC-447`. |

| AC-291 | the history of `dev` is separate and has one commit for each private commit | `scripts/public-sync/tests/engine.test.mjs` | Over a private repo and a public clone built in a temporary folder: `dev` has one root commit with the scrubbed tree and the files the public repo owns (its README stays the public one, the method's files are absent, a locale file is as it was); each later private commit that changes something public is one public commit with the private author and the task's description as its message, and a private commit that touches only denied files makes none; a second run changes nothing. Tests: `AC-291 dev has a root commit with the scrubbed tree and the files the public repo owns`, `AC-291 dev has one commit for each private commit that changes something public`, `AC-291 a second run changes nothing`. |
| AC-292 | the wording of the method is rewritten and an id leaves no artefact | `scripts/public-sync/tests/scrub.test.mjs` | A sentence in the map is replaced by its plain wording and the rest of its paragraph is kept; ids leave no empty bracket, no dangling "see", no stray comma and no empty comment; a sentence that names the method and is not in the map is reported (and kept, so the guard stops the run, unless dropping is allowed); locale and lock files are not touched; a commit message goes through the same map. Tests: `AC-292 a mapped paragraph is rewritten`, `AC-292 ids leave no artefact`, `AC-292 an unmapped paragraph is reported, and kept unless dropping is allowed`, `AC-292 locale files and lock files are not touched`, `AC-292 a commit message is rewritten through the same map`, `AC-292 a key is one line with the spaces collapsed`. |
| AC-293 | the owner's remarks are rewritten as feature statements | `scripts/public-sync/tests/scrub.test.mjs` | "The owner asked for X" becomes "Provides X"; what is left of the word names the teacher; a comment that mentions the owner comes out without the word. Tests: `AC-293 an owner remark becomes a feature statement`, `AC-293 a comment that mentions the owner comes out without the word`. |
| AC-294 | only the denied paths are removed and the closure check refuses a missing file | `scripts/public-sync/tests/closure.test.mjs`, `scripts/public-sync/tests/engine.test.mjs` | The method's files are denied and ordinary ones (README, docs, scripts, the app) are not; a relative import, a stylesheet url or a script a package runs that points at a file the tree does not hold is a finding naming both files; an import that is there as a file, an index or a partial passes; a public file whose import goes to a denied file stops the sync. Tests: `AC-294 only the denied paths are removed`, `AC-294 a missing import fails the closure`, `AC-294 an import that is there, as a file, an index or a partial, passes`, `AC-294 a missing asset fails the closure`, `AC-294 a script a package runs must be in the tree`, `AC-294 the files the public repo owns are kept in the list of what it owns`, `AC-294 a file the public tree needs and does not hold stops the run`. |
| AC-295 | the public commit message is derived from the task description and is clean | `scripts/public-sync/tests/guard.test.mjs` | The message is the task's description with a one-line subject and no id, session or method word; a commit with no task (or one whose own subject is the method's words) gets a subject from its paths; a description that names the method is reported. Tests: `AC-295 the message comes from the task description`, `AC-295 a commit with no task gets a subject from its paths`, `AC-295 a description that names the method is reported, not published`. |
| AC-296 | the leak guard stops a leak and passes a clean tree | `scripts/public-sync/tests/guard.test.mjs`, `scripts/public-sync/tests/engine.test.mjs` | A method word, an id, a token-shaped string, a person's path and a denied file are each found with file, line and rule and never the value; a tree of ordinary files and a locale file passes; messages with a method word or an id are findings; after a stop the public branch has not moved, and an unmapped sentence stops the run until dropping it is chosen. Tests: `AC-296 each kind of leak is found`, `AC-296 a clean tree passes`, `AC-296 a message with a method word or an id is a finding`, `AC-296 a leak stops the run and the branch does not move`, `AC-296 an unmapped sentence stops the run, and dropping it is the owner's explicit choice`. |
| AC-297 | the hooks run on main only and the command line works by hand | `scripts/public-sync/tests/hooks.test.mjs` | The two hooks are in the repo, check the branch is main, run the sync quietly and always exit 0; run in a repository they run the sync on main and not on another branch; `status` and `scan` write nothing to the public repo. Tests: `AC-297 the hooks are in the repo, run the sync on main only and never fail a commit`, `AC-297 the hook runs the sync on main and not on another branch`, `AC-297 status and scan change nothing`. |
| AC-298 | the intake lists a pull request's changes and never merges | `scripts/public-sync/tests/hooks.test.mjs` | For a branch of the public clone the intake lists the files and commits against `dev`, drafts a row for each area and prints the steps; its source writes and merges nothing. Tests: `AC-298 the intake lists the changes and the checklist`, `AC-298 the intake leaves the private repo untouched`. |
